# Anevia — round 2 set-piece sheet

Planning only, 2026-10-06. Baseline: `219bcbebe010c892d8532b68b83183958b0e1ec3`; turning-point sheet read directly from `origin/claude/tp-anevia` at `c3219d714a9636e5300ce7254f17a9b83b929bda`. No runtime source, generated export, choices, conditions, prices or relationship state changed. All proposed encounters, room details, watch discrepancies and romance dialogue below are **authored additions**. Native citations establish character, event and hook; they do not establish a native romance.

## Binding design and evidence

Read: POLISH-AGENT-PROMPT steps 1–3; 00-WRITING-GUIDE; all ROUND2-TURNING-POINTS revisions; CANON-PARTNERS-DESIGN including stance revision; TRICKSTER-RUBRIC including contexts (1)–(5), foresight and DLC rewrites; this route's atlas reservation, atlas-0005 and ADOPT/ADAPT TIR rows; Anevia's Trickster spec; the three implemented Anevia modules; her existing Story.json nodes and shared Tirabade dependencies. Heat inventory below includes generated partner variants and conditional aftermath paragraphs rather than assuming the base scene covers its copies.

**Reservation, unchanged:** `she_pursues`; Chapter 5; **Drezen outer gate, smith side of the cart ruts at watch change**; payoff **spy chooses rendezvous and keeps nights at her marital home**. The paid dead-drop approach earns a hearing. Anevia arrives first, chooses the next meeting, then makes her own intimate offer. Ordinary Ch3 courtship remains its existing road, not a newly required prologue to the returned route. Public Iz accounting remains confined to the history that already owes it.

**Character:** a sharp, suspicious scout with a dirty joke ready when she is uncomfortable; she enjoys the Commander's unpredictability and knows the cost of being lied to. Her desire does not replace her loyalty to Beth. She may lie to get a night she wants, but owns the lie when caught. Her decisive counter-move is **taking control of the rendezvous**: the Commander reaches her; she closes that access and determines where another hearing happens. During ordinary courtship the equivalent is taking an hour and a room for herself, not being awarded to the Commander for solving paperwork.

**Exclusions:** no potion, drunken turning point, staged rescue, new forged proof, affection currency, universal public confession, extra reconciliation condition, new return device, or echo. The watch discrepancy is genuine evidence of a personal lie, not a counterfeit operational record. Kiana's mid-act husband interruption stays hers. No Shyka page earns Anevia's desire, knowledge or forgiveness. No new registry write is permitted here.

## Six set pieces

### 1. Reports finished; she has not gone home — entry

**Where/when:** Ch3 Drezen, existing private room after reports, seeded by `a_cup` → `a_errand` → `a_roof` → `a_crossing`; ordinary alternative `anevia.unborrowed_hour` → `anevia.a_question_at_home`. Ch1–2 contact establishes the marriage and her habit of noticing everything, not a sexual obligation for tending her broken leg.

**Present/risk:** Anevia and Commander; Beth is at home only when her actual state supports it. Dispatch traffic remains audible. Her desire risks the trust of the woman who held Kenabres with her. No imaginary wife's absence.

**Build-up/beats:** preserve the prior invitation. She has put the reports away but waited after her work ended. She names Beth before touching the Commander. On the honest road she goes home to ask her wife and arranges another conversation. On the existing affair road she admits Beth has not agreed, takes the Commander's face and kisses, freezes at footsteps, then chooses whether to finish the night. Replace repeated face-reading/last-chance negotiation with that one visible decision and existing stop/leave choices. Her spy reflex catches a passing runner; this is the near-discovery, not Beth barging into a sex act. The scarf/laces and subsequent return home belong to the same risky night.

**Her answer/options:** Anevia chooses the kiss and separately the night. Commander stays, stops after the kiss, leaves, or asks for the honest spouse conversation. The existing `a_affair` also covers a stopped kiss: narration must not turn that history into intercourse. Append no required flirt score or new gate.

**Payoff/carry:** `a_crossing.explicit.1` bridges the existing undressing and later dressing only on the stayed-night passage. `a_morning` makes her appetite and lie coexist; she cannot joke away Beth's supper. TIR-02 adopted. `a_will_tell` remains a promise to disclose, not disclosure itself. A kiss earns no household commitment. Shared disclosure scenes remain their owner's work. C1/C2/C4 supply the marriage and spy reflex; the romance is authored.

### 2. The scout's hour is eaten by a real case — build-up

**Where/when:** existing Ch3 Drezen street/market and investigation locations; `anevia.borrowed_signature` through `anevia.what_the_warning_cost`, then `anevia.the_evening_without_a_case`. No new civic quest or second fraud chain.

**Present/risk:** Anevia, Commander and already implemented witnesses Ressa/Dema only where the scene supplies them. A courier's safety, the usable evidence and her promised personal evening are at risk while the crusade depends on reliable dispatches. These names/details are authored, not native canon.

**Build-up/beats:** Anevia runs the investigation; Commander helps or declines through existing answers. She corrects a witness by asking what else happened that day, not by inventing an alibi for her. Preserve warning/evidence consequences. At the already existing kept/rescheduled evening, a runner's actual watch timing nearly contradicts her private account; Anevia notices before the Commander and cuts off the work talk. If she is being honest, she names her evening plainly. If she is concealing an affair, the uncomfortable precision is hers. No forgery, new messenger event, mandatory failure, or romance reward for a solved case.

**Her answer/options:** her counter-move is to reschedule herself and follow through, or to insist that the case wait while she keeps the existing evening. Commander keeps the evening, keeps a different promise, or chooses quiet company. She draws the Commander from the goat game toward the bed because she wants them; the case did not supply that answer.

**Payoff/carry:** `anevia.the_evening_without_a_case.explicit.1` for the stayed-night branch; sleep-only keeps its own warm morning without sexual implication. After intimacy, she steals breakfast, checks the watch and goes home on Beth's night. TIR-01 adapted as her existing work, without importing another false-itinerary con; TIR-04 excludes rescue-as-marriage-repair. Evidence/buyer/wooden-goat callbacks require the already played producer. Hook C4; contact at Anevia's native hub; all investigation/room details authored.

### 3. Two reports, one marriage — stance, discovery and confrontation

**Where/when:** the existing spouse interviews in Ch3 (`anevia.beths_question`, `anevia.beths_answer`), stance decision in Ch5 `anevia.a_key_that_is_hers`; exposure in `anevia.the_last_ordinary_thing/partner_discovered` or returned commitment/retry's existing dawn discovery. One confrontation staged at the current discovery beat, not an additional event chain. If Beth is away, use existing supported correspondence and retain that absence; no bodily scene by mail.

**Present/risk:** both wives and Commander only with earned physical presence. Beth's military accuracy and marriage trust are separate stakes. She remains an investigator and paladin with duties, not a jealous obstacle or a transferable invitation.

**Share:** Anevia says she wants the Commander and is keeping her house with Beth. Beth faces the Commander herself: rank buys no right to her marriage; the wives' nights and house remain theirs. Preserve her actual spoken/signed answer and refusal. No demand that Beth become the Commander's lover. Her own move is to separate truthful duty reports from the private arrangement and keep the agreed nights herself.

**Exclusive:** Commander can demand that Anevia leave Beth; Anevia blocks the hand at her belt and refuses. Commander backs down to existing share terms or insists and loses/cools this romance through its existing exit. CANON-PARTNERS' route-specific refusal controls over the general TIR-S living-separation suggestion. Do not create an Anevia divorce reward.

**Secret/near-discovery:** keep her guilty choice and the established missed supper/dispatch pretext. The runner/watch detail in pieces 1–2 can unsettle it before discovery without producing a new state requirement. Beth finds that the claimed work interval does not match the real dispatch/watch; an alibi too neat is recognizable as her wife's handiwork. No magical lie detector, new forged sheet or command coercion.

**Discovery/confrontation:** replace the off-page summary with TIR-03/TIR-06 at the existing exposure node. Beth lays down the accurate operational report and the private account of that evening. She will not falsify the watch list and will not stage a court spectacle. She asks which of them decided she would be easiest to manage. Anevia starts explaining the beginning; Beth asks how long. Anevia gives the duration supported by this history, then owns wanting the Commander. Commander can admit their part or use the already available lie posture; if an additional answer is needed it is appended and lands on the existing exposure/fallout, with no new effect or forgiveness roll. Calling the affair operationally necessary is treated as a lie, not a v1 manipulation device.

**Her answer/partner's move:** Anevia goes home to Beth. Beth bars the Commander from their house and supplies the necessary patrol reports anyway. Existing exposed-secret ending remains marriage survives/Commander unwelcome. Beth's possible personal attraction belongs to her own played route; never invent or award it here. A living but unreconciled Beth is not a corpse; a reconciled but departed Beth is not in the room.

**Payoff/carry:** secret desire has a night and an ugly dawn; no instant reset to share. Ordinary secret, absent-wife secret and gate secret retain their different current consequences. TIR-02/03/04/06 adopted; TIR-R deferred to the already owned return device; TIR-W adds no widow mechanism. Household, endings and Last Call read `anevia.partner_stance.{share|exclusive|secret}`, `anevia.partner_lie_exposed`, `anevia.partner.absence_kept` and current wife state, not a kiss. C1/C2/C4; interviews on existing Irabeth hub. Shared staging/Irabeth-route effects require coordination.

### 4. She arrives before the watch changes — registered turn

**Where/when:** Ch5 after actual contact/return, outer Drezen gate, smith side of the cart ruts. `anevia.trickster.gone.gate` → `gone.commit`; fetched clones preserve their own clock. The approach in `gone.setup`/`gone.wardrobe`, fetched journey or confession is setup, not this turn.

**Present/risk:** Anevia and Commander; smith/guards remain working background, not invented confidants. Beth participates only through an earned present encounter or existing letter; true death is remembered. Anevia risks entering a relationship without letting the citadel, Commander or demon take possession of her movements.

**Build-up/beats:** paid closet, kept door and supply crate retain their distinct costs and actual intrusion. She keeps the knife, bars the wardrobe and sends the Commander away with her own appointment. At the gate she is already waiting on the road side. Let her watch the change of sentries, pick a spot where the smith's hammer hides her quieter words and explain the real-door terms. Commander has reached her; now she decides where they may come close. No invented operational bribe.

**Her answer/options:** truthful account and existing terms create the chance, never the yes. She moves the lantern herself and calls the Commander to her. Commander accepts her invitation, waits, declines or leaves. Living-wife stances route through piece 3; the wife's signed share terms stay legible before undressing. True widow retains grief. Unrecovered player-killed-wife history preserves prior lover, private admission and played public muster before later intimacy; paying muster buys a hearing, not pardon. The current muster/late-derived shortcuts are listed for runtime repair, not implemented here. Deliberate closure is legitimate.

**Payoff/carry:** the selected existing invitation leads through a real door to the gatehouse cloak; slot at `threshold`, not a new rescue night. Retry retains one true secret, paid once, justified by the actual approach rather than a wardrobe in fetched history. Commander can fail or be refused. Returned acceptance does not silently move Anevia into Drezen or dissolve her marriage. C3–C7; smith/native-unit staging above. No page/echo or vision excuse.

**Clock:** ordinary magical schedule is currently 24 + 48 + 96 = 168h; fetched 96 + 12 + 6 = 114h, retry 120h. Crate's stated three days south needs the northbound journey too; do not call 24h a physical return. Over-budget widow/killer retries use the already proposed postwar door continuation for coordinator review, not compressed grief or a new teleport. This sheet does not change any delay.

### 5. The bed is hers; the home is still theirs — payoff and distinct dawns

**Where/when:** Ch5 existing lease room `anevia.a_key_that_is_hers`; returned alternative gatehouse, or Commander's real door on `gone.second_ask/night`. A delayed postwar invitation uses the promised existing destination, never a letter that grants arrival.

**Present/risk:** Anevia and Commander. In a separately earned joint history, both wives invite at `three_open_road`/`three_rooms_unlocked`; their marriage is an active source of desire. No wife is brought in as a gift. Scout duty, kept nights and the difference between a lease and abandonment remain tangible.

**Build-up/beats:** ordinary route has had an actual private evening, absence and reunion. The lease scene asks whether the Commander will keep returning; she keeps her own key. That decision and stance precede the night. At the bed she stops a practical explanation with a kiss, swears at her own knots and draws the Commander close. Do not make this another first night after the Ch3 night. Gate and retry are first nights only in histories where no prior night happened; otherwise they are renewed intimacy. Avoid deriving a new mandatory gate from this editorial distinction.

**Her answer/options:** existing shared-key/kept-invitations/open/parted exits remain. Share, refusal of exclusive and culpable secret follow piece 3. The sleep-only branch is intimacy on its own terms. The Commander's refusal does not make her promise a different woman. In a joint scene both wives independently reach for each other and the Commander; a negotiated table alone does not grant a joint sexual invitation.

**Aftermath:** ordinary dawn keeps her wearing the Commander's shirt while she laces boots, steals food, and returns to Beth. Secret dawn keeps the packed supper/actual lie and no reassuring promise of eventual forgiveness. Away-wife secret keeps a straight letter still unwritten, and no new discovery guarantee. Gate dawn distinguishes Beth alive/reconciled, alive/unreconciled, departed and dead; the widow's portrait is not a generic prop for living-wife variants. Retry's cold bed and “Probably” note follow her chosen visit; show the next actual return in the coda, rather than treating absence as devotion. No tender night forgives Iz.

**Payoff/carry:** slot map below covers ordinary/secret/absent/gate/retry/muster and fetched copies individually. Split their existing passages at the act boundary; preserve the following paragraph/choice and all Set/Next mechanics. C1/C2 anchor the marriage, C5 the grief. Rooms, lease, breakfast and intimacy are authored. Shared joint nights require the shared owner, not edits by this task.

### 6. She goes on the night operation and comes back — aftermath

**Where/when:** existing Ch5 farewell `anevia.the_last_ordinary_thing`, current ending pages and postwar/Last Call coda. At the Tirabade home only with an actual welcome and living present wives; otherwise use the route's already chosen room/road or absence/remembrance.

**Present/risk:** earned living Anevia, Commander only if alive/returned, Beth only if present. The crusade's surviving work and the wives' continuing marriage have a future separate from romance. No helper or creditor appears from an availability label alone.

**Build-up/beats:** TIR-05 adapted to the existing coda: an operational case is briefly on the table, Anevia steals a bite, objects to the Commander's guess, leaves for her work and returns in the same coda after the watch. Beth keeps supper/gets up for her own duty and answers in her own voice where present. This is a return the player sees, not a departing note that sets arrival. Exposed affair uses professional contact and Anevia returning to her wife, without the Commander's domestic welcome. No obligatory shared fraud case or new mission.

**Her answer/options:** her kept invitation is shown by the return; the Commander can meet it or retain the existing open/parted outcome. No new ending choice or mechanics. Joint TIR-07 meal is wives-initiated only in the already earned joint history; it cannot upgrade a solo affair to three lovers.

**Payoff/carry:** share keeps the wives' nights; exclusive insistence keeps refusal; exposed secret keeps the barred house; absent-wife secret acknowledges the still-live marriage and unresolved secrecy; grief remembers Beth; unrecovered departure/death stays absent; an unreturned fatal sacrifice has no living Commander breakfast. Retained closet is not a listening debt; paid listening names a creditor only when present. `cost.lie_exposed` (Iz story) and `partner_lie_exposed` (affair) are different wrongs. Native slides receive only Trickster-dependent changes explained by actual return. C8/C9. Last Call/shared household/native-slide integration is escalation, not work performed here.

## Explicit-slot handoff

These are **planning addresses**, not appended runtime nodes. Writers stage desire and the initiating motion; the default remains a heated cut. Briefs supply non-graphic situation, voice, continuity, partner consequence and a fixed landing for a later user-supplied passage. They do not generate explicit prose. Native facts: Anevia is a woman, scout and Irabeth's wife; no invented measurements, genital configuration, surgical detail or fetish biography. Commander variants are explicit because future anatomy-dependent fills must support either variant without altering her canon. Every brief carries only `A`/`B` NPC speakers plus `N`; Commander dialogue remains selectable answers outside the slot.

Numbering is local to each scene. Each JSON has the exact default and landing, its source node, following destination and handoff constraints. `.explicit.2`/`.3` are alternative stance nights, not repeated acts on a single traversal.

No explicit slot for a rescue, leg splint, refused kiss, grief conversation, simple sleep, breakfast, exposition, or epilogue recap. Shared slot briefs are reviewable proposals under this route's allowed planning directory; their insertion belongs to the shared owner. No new witnesses or discovery interruption inside a slot. A dawn discovery begins after the supplied landing, never as a Kiana-style knock in the middle.

## Heat audit: disposition rules and completeness

Ratings are editorial fit estimates, not independent audit scores: **strong 91–95**, **revise 80–90**, **weak 65–79**. Passing kisses are listed honestly rather than manufacturing defects. Sex thresholds may have strong build-up but still need a slot where the abrupt transition loses the scene. The full addressed inventory follows the implementation checklist; quotes are existing text, not replacements. Each row maps to a situation-level instruction below or a specific slot. Paragraph addresses `node/paragraph[n]` are zero-based existing positions for review only, not proposed saved IDs.

**H1 — ordinary touch/kiss, 88–94:** keep voluntary, concrete contact and her impatience; shorten repeated permission checks and interchangeable forehead/cheek gestures. At the particular street/table/door, let a dispatch, approaching boots or her own unfinished joke determine timing. Her decision is a physical action; player stop/near exits remain. No slot for every kiss.

**H2 — act threshold, 82 for the jump, 92–95 for existing appetite:** use the mapped slot exactly at the current initiating motion; keep laces, swearing, awkward furniture and the existing aftermath. Replace summary “Later”/instant dawn with a continuous act-boundary handoff. New text cannot decide stance, marriage, arrival, public confession or commitment. Do not turn later nights into second first nights.

**H3 — mornings, 85–94:** make her boots/shirt, food theft, watch and real destination carry the night. Share goes home on the kept night; secret faces the supper lie; absent-wife affair carries an unwritten letter; widow carries grief without restoring Beth. A cold bed is followed by a witnessed return in the coda. Quiet sleep remains quiet sleep. No invented hangover, injury or additional cost.

**H4 — partner terms/fallout, 82–95:** preserve the frank refusal and ugly rows; replace sole off-page discovery summary with piece 3's genuine watch evidence, two reports and Beth's own move at the existing discovery beat. A signed letter stays a letter if Beth is not present. Keep both wives' wanting without humiliation framing, divorce pressure succeeding automatically or command rank buying acceptance.

**H5 — future/grief/ending/Last Call, 80–94:** use actual played nights and partner state. Show one concrete return when alive/welcome; keep refusal, absence, memory and barred-house outcomes distinct. No sex in ending recaps. Existing romantic key/goat/portrait imagery is conditional on its producer; Aeon tavern romance is a canon defect requiring C9 restoration. Last Call's creditor paragraphs distinguish listening, wrong-door and retained-door histories. Shared-owner escalation applies.

**H6 — joint warmth, 84–94:** let wives take the initiative toward each other, with their existing appetites and duties; affection between them is not a guarantee about the Commander. Preserve warm nonsexual branches. At the two actual joint-night thresholds use the shared-slot proposal; morning keeps the three distinct people and departure time. No joint yes derived from table/chair attendance. Shared owner only.

**H0 — deliberately nonsexual anchor, 94:** injury care, scarf keepsake, marriage recollection or professional concern may contain physical words. Preserve the actual context; do not eroticize rescue, medical care or bereavement to raise a heat count. Such inventory rows mark scope, not a demand for sexual content.

## Outcomes that must differ

| History | Scene/ending consequence, without changing mechanics |
|---|---|
| Honest share, solo Anevia | Her invitation and kept return; Beth's spoken/signed terms; marital home retained; Beth need not romance Commander. |
| Separately earned joint romance | Each wife answers, pursues and reaches for the other; shared domestic invitation and actual return; not solo share upgraded by a chair. |
| Exclusive demand, insist | Anevia refuses, romance closes/cools through existing exit; Beth remains wife. Backdown is the existing share road. |
| Secret, discovered | Genuine watch discrepancy; two reports; Anevia owns desire and goes home; marriage survives, Commander unwelcome. No forgiveness roll or restored share night. |
| Secret while Beth away | Affair and still-live wife acknowledged; present absence/return reality retained; no mail that conjures Beth or new forced discovery mechanism. |
| Former affair, honest disclosure | Kiss-only/night histories described accurately; played disclosure pays the promise; later wanting is not amnesty for the original lie. |
| Returned widow, prior romance | Grief survives renewed desire; no substituted wife's bed; her later yes required; no new widow-only reward/return mechanism. |
| Unrecovered player-killed wife | Existing prior lover/admission/played public muster and her later decision; stranger gets friendship/refusal. Deliberate closure stands. |
| Paid access, refused/friends only | Contact/work/company, no commitment or household romance from payment, rescue, hand-taking or Shyka page. |
| Anevia dead or departed without earned return | Absence/remembrance, no bodily household/Last Call/room return. |
| Commander fatal sacrifice without return | Bereavement, no living kitchen or bed page. |

## Ordered round 2 implementation checklist — future owner work

1. Freeze scene/node/relationship IDs, GuidFor namespace, original order and all choice indices, including generated fetched/partner variants. Preserve original newline bytes. Append answers/nodes only; retire by existing/approved conditions, retaining destinations. Legacy ending exit identity, Set and Next remain unchanged.
2. Compare source against export; repair the turning sheet's already named acceptance shortcuts before adding intimacy: late hand-derived commitment, muster choice implying an unplayed admission/payment, and any payoff skipping owed prior-lover/public-account history. Do not add new price, reconciliation test, gate or attraction condition.
3. Stage pieces 1–2 inside their existing scenes. Keep case consequences independent of desire, historical kiss-only branches accurate, and first/renewed nights distinct using existing played history. Do not add a forged itinerary or turn the case into a new romance gate.
4. Stage piece 3 at existing stance/exposure nodes and clones; preserve stance flag producers and the final fallout. Append any necessary admission/lie answer at the end and retain the existing consequence. Shared confrontation/Irabeth effects go to coordinator. Exclusive stays refused; no divorce branch.
5. Stage registered gate pursuit using real return/presence and the existing timing. Repair only timing/false callbacks identified by the TP findings. Fetched/crate/confession cannot recall a wardrobe encounter they never had. Over-budget continuation proposal is reviewed by coordinator before runtime work.
6. Insert approved slots from this sheet at the designated paragraph boundary, with default text playable and the exact JSON landing adjoining existing aftermath. No slot flags, new mechanics or inferred yes. For terminal branches preserve every old answer's effects; for nested mornings keep all original conditional destinations.
7. Rewrite mornings by H3 and partner outcomes by H4; audit every fetched clone, retry/muster variant and absence/returned/dead branch. No living-wife grave/portrait language. No repeat first-night reward or automatic reconciliation.
8. Apply H5/H6 to route endings and coordinate shared household/Last Call/joint-night edits. Preserve native slides elsewhere; Trickster-only rewrite only the fact explained by the earned event. Replace Aeon tavern fiction with native C9 fact. Carry wife/Commander current presence and creditor presence explicitly.
9. Review narrative paths against the outcomes table: honest/secret/exclusive/backdown, kiss-only/night/sleep-only, widow/killer/stranger, all three approach mechanisms, fetched/confession, spouse alive-unreconciled/departed/away/dead, refusal/late invitation, sacrifice/return and off-Trickster canon. These are future review histories, not tests run in this planning task.
10. Coordinator performs global uniqueness registration check and later runtime verification. This task runs no expansion, builds, tests, verifier or rules gate and makes no commit, following its task-specific hard rule and final no-commit instruction.

## ESCALATE / PROPOSE / RISKS for coordinator

**ESCALATE:** every runtime insertion is outside this planning-only allowlist. In particular shared `a_*`, `three_*`, joint table/chair, household and Last Call belong to their owners; the two-reports confrontation must not be implemented twice. Native epilogue/partner copies need the same narrow Trickster correction as their source. The TP sheet's acceptance/presence/timing/Aeon defects remain pending runtime work; this plan does not certify them fixed. Global turning-point registry remains untouched.

**PROPOSE, not implemented:** use the TP sheet's one postwar real-door continuation for over-budget histories, after checking that it does not add a new gate/price. No new spouse fracture, rescue, proof con, romantic score, potion or echo is proposed.

**RISKS:** stale generated exports can hide source-only amendments; source and export must be compared before insertion. Generated stance variants multiply the prose sweep. Exposure-at-dawn cannot contain a present Beth if the itinerary puts her elsewhere. Briefs are situation/continuity handoffs; later filled passages require voice, anatomy-variant and transition review and are not runtime-validated here. No independent rubric score is claimed.
